def max_points_no_iso_trap(n):
    # n x n grid with coordinates 1..n
    N=n*n
    # variable index mapping (i,j) to idx
    idx=lambda i,j: (i-1)*n+(j-1)  # i row (y), j column (x)
    # generate constraints as list of lists of indices that cannot all be 1
    constraints=[]
    # horizontal base
    for y1 in range(1,n+1):
        for y2 in range(y1+1,n+1):
            for x1 in range(1,n+1):
                for x2 in range(1,n+1):
                    if x1==x2: continue
                    for x3 in range(1,n+1):
                        for x4 in range(1,n+1):
                            if x3==x4: continue
                            # check condition
                            if abs(x1 - x3) == abs(x2 - x4):
                                # add constraint: these four cannot all be selected
                                constraints.append([idx(y1,x1), idx(y1,x2), idx(y2,x3), idx(y2,x4)])
    # vertical base (swap x and y)
    for x1 in range(1,n+1):
        for x2 in range(x1+1,n+1):
            for y1 in range(1,n+1):
                for y2 in range(1,n+1):
                    if y1==y2: continue
                    for y3 in range(1,n+1):
                        for y4 in range(1,n+1):
                            if y3==y4: continue
                            if abs(y1 - y3) == abs(y2 - y4):
                                constraints.append([idx(y1,x1), idx(y2,x1), idx(y3,x2), idx(y4,x2)])
    # Use MILP: maximize sum of variables, binary, each constraint sum <=3
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    c = -np.ones(N)  # maximize sum => minimize negative sum
    A = np.zeros((len(constraints), N), dtype=int)
    for i, quad in enumerate(constraints):
        for v in quad:
            A[i, v] = 1
    lc = LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, 3)  # sum <=3
    bounds = Bounds(0, 1)
    integrality = np.ones(N, dtype=int)
    res = milp(c, constraints=[lc], bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("failed")
        return None
    val = -res.fun
    # round
    return int(round(val))

for n in range(2,5):
    print(n, max_points_no_iso_trap(n))

def max_points_no_iso_trap(n):
    # n x n grid with coordinates 1..n
    N=n*n
    # variable index mapping (i,j) to idx
    idx=lambda i,j: (i-1)*n+(j-1)  # i row (y), j column (x)
    # generate constraints as list of lists of indices that cannot all be 1
    constraints=[]
    # horizontal base
    for y1 in range(1,n+1):
        for y2 in range(y1+1,n+1):
            for x1 in range(1,n+1):
                for x2 in range(1,n+1):
                    if x1==x2: continue
                    for x3 in range(1,n+1):
                        for x4 in range(1,n+1):
                            if x3==x4: continue
                            # check condition
                            if abs(x1 - x3) == abs(x2 - x4):
                                # add constraint: these four cannot all be selected
                                constraints.append([idx(y1,x1), idx(y1,x2), idx(y2,x3), idx(y2,x4)])
    # vertical base (swap x and y)
    for x1 in range(1,n+1):
        for x2 in range(x1+1,n+1):
            for y1 in range(1,n+1):
                for y2 in range(1,n+1):
                    if y1==y2: continue
                    for y3 in range(1,n+1):
                        for y4 in range(1,n+1):
                            if y3==y4: continue
                            if abs(y1 - y3) == abs(y2 - y4):
                                constraints.append([idx(y1,x1), idx(y2,x1), idx(y3,x2), idx(y4,x2)])
    # Use MILP: maximize sum of variables, binary, each constraint sum <=3
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    c = -np.ones(N)  # maximize sum => minimize negative sum
    A = np.zeros((len(constraints), N), dtype=int)
    for i, quad in enumerate(constraints):
        for v in quad:
            A[i, v] = 1
    lc = LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, 3)  # sum <=3
    bounds = Bounds(0, 1)
    integrality = np.ones(N, dtype=int)
    res = milp(c, constraints=[lc], bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("failed")
        return None
    val = -res.fun
    # round
    return int(round(val))

for n in range(2,5):
    print(n, max_points_no_iso_trap(n))

[CODE] (turn 2):
def find_perm(N):
    # brute force using permutations of range(N)
    # but for N>8, huge. We'll test small N.
    for perm in itertools.permutations(range(1,N+1)):
        diffs = [abs(perm[i]- (i+1)) for i in range(N)]
        # we need all diffs distinct and cover 1..N-1 (maybe 0 allowed if fixed point)
        # we require no i such that perm[i]==i+1? Actually we can allow one fixed point maybe.
        # For our construction we need each row upgraded (except maybe one) has a distinct diff.
        # We'll just check if set(diffs)==set(range(1,N)):
        if set(diffs)==set(range(1,N)):
            return perm
    return None

for N in range(2,9):
    perm = find_perm(N)
    print(N, perm)

def find_perm(N):
    # brute force using permutations of range(N)
    # but for N>8, huge. We'll test small N.
    for perm in itertools.permutations(range(1,N+1)):
        diffs = [abs(perm[i]- (i+1)) for i in range(N)]
        # we need all diffs distinct and cover 1..N-1 (maybe 0 allowed if fixed point)
        # we require no i such that perm[i]==i+1? Actually we can allow one fixed point maybe.
        # For our construction we need each row upgraded (except maybe one) has a distinct diff.
        # We'll just check if set(diffs)==set(range(1,N)):
        if set(diffs)==set(range(1,N)):
            return perm
    return None

[CODE] (turn 3):
def find_perm_one_fixed(N):
    for perm in itertools.permutations(range(1,N+1)):
        fixed = [i+1 for i,p in enumerate(perm) if p==i+1]
        if len(fixed)!=1:
            continue
        diffs = [abs(perm[i]-(i+1)) for i in range(N) if perm[i]!=(i+1)]
        if set(diffs)==set(range(1,N)):
            return perm, fixed[0]
    return None

for N in range(2,9):
    res = find_perm_one_fixed(N)
    print(N, res)

[CODE] (turn 6):
def construct_points(N):
    # find permutation with one fixed point and distinct diffs
    perm, fixed = find_perm_one_fixed_bruteforce(N)
    points = set()
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        points.add((i,i))
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        if i != fixed:
            points.add((i, perm[i-1]))
    return points, fixed, perm

def check_constraints(points, N):
    pts = list(points)
    # check for any isosceles trapezoid with horizontal bases
    # iterate over pairs of rows
    rows = {}
    for (x,y) in pts:
        rows.setdefault(y, []).append(x)
    for y1 in rows:
        for y2 in rows:
            if y1 >= y2: continue
            xs1 = rows[y1]
            xs2 = rows[y2]
            # need at least two points in each row
            if len(xs1) < 2 or len(xs2) < 2: continue
            # check all pairs
            for i in range(len(xs1)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(xs1)):
                    a = xs1[i]; b = xs1[j]
                    for p in range(len(xs2)):
                        for q in range(p+1, len(xs2)):
                            c = xs2[p]; d = xs2[q]
                            if abs(a - b) == abs(c - d):
                                # we have isosceles trapezoid (difference same)
                                return False
    # similarly for columns
    cols = {}
    for (x,y) in pts:
        cols.setdefault(x, []).append(y)
    for x1 in cols:
        for x2 in cols:
            if x1 >= x2: continue
            ys1 = cols[x1]
            ys2 = cols[x2]
            if len(ys1) < 2 or len(ys2) < 2: continue
            for i in range(len(ys1)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(ys1)):
                    a = ys1[i]; b = ys1[j]
                    for p in range(len(ys2)):
                        for q in range(p+1, len(ys2)):
                            c = ys2[p]; d = ys2[q]
                            if abs(a - b) == abs(c - d):
                                return False
    return True

for N in range(4,11):
    pts, fixed, perm = construct_points(N)
    ok = check_constraints(pts, N)
    print(N, len(pts), ok)

def max_points(N):
    res = find_perm_one_fixed_bruteforce(N)
    if res is None:
        return None
    perm, fixed = res
    pts = set()
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        pts.add((i,i))
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        if i != fixed:
            pts.add((i, perm[i-1]))
    return len(pts), check_constraints(pts,N)

for N in range(4,11):
    result = max_points(N)
    print(N, result)

[CODE] (turn 3):
def generate_constraints(N):
    # returns list of tuples (indices list, rhs) for sum <= 3
    # index mapping: (i,j) -> i*N + j
    constraints = []
    # horizontal bases
    for y1 in range(N):
        for y2 in range(y1+1, N):
            dy = y2 - y1
            # choose a,b top row columns (order matters? a<b)
            for a in range(N):
                for b in range(a+1, N):
                    # need c,d bottom row such that |a-c| = |b-d|
                    # possibilities: c = a + t, d = b + t with t integer (could be negative) and within range
                    # also could be c = a - t, d = b - t (same as t negative)
                    # So we can iterate over t such that both c,d in [0,N-1]
                    for t in range(-N, N):
                        c = a + t
                        d = b + t
                        if 0 <= c < N and 0 <= d < N:
                            # ensure c<d? Not necessary; but bottom base should have c<d (order). Let's enforce c<d to avoid duplicate.
                            if c < d:
                                # add constraint for four points
                                idx = [y1*N + a, y1*N + b, y2*N + c, y2*N + d]
                                constraints.append(idx)
    # vertical bases
    for x1 in range(N):
        for x2 in range(x1+1, N):
            dx = x2 - x1
            for a in range(N):
                for b in range(a+1, N):
                    # a,b are y coordinates on left column x1? Actually points (x1,a), (x1,b) left base
                    # Need c,d on right column x2 such that |a-c| = |b-d|
                    for t in range(-N, N):
                        c = a + t
                        d = b + t
                        if 0 <= c < N and 0 <= d < N and c < d:
                            idx = [a*N + x1, b*N + x1, c*N + x2, d*N + x2]
                            constraints.append(idx)
    return constraints

def solve_max(N):
    constraints = generate_constraints(N)
    m = N*N
    # objective: maximize sum of x_i
    c = -np.ones(m)  # maximize => minimize -sum
    # Build A_ub matrix: each constraint sum <=3 => sum - 3 <= 0? Actually we need sum <= 3, so sum - 3 <= 0.
    # We'll create A_ub * x <= b_ub
    A = []
    b = []
    for idx in constraints:
        row = np.zeros(m, dtype=int)
        row[idx] = 1
        A.append(row)
        b.append(3)
    A = np.array(A, dtype=int)
    b = np.array(b, dtype=int)
    # variable bounds 0 <= x <=1, integer
    from scipy.optimize import milp, Bounds, LinearConstraint, OptimizeResult
    bounds = Bounds(0, 1)
    # integrality vector
    integrality = np.ones(m, dtype=int)
    lincon = LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, b)  # A x <= b
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=lincon, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("MILP failed")
        return None
    val = -res.fun  # because we minimized -sum
    # rounding due to tolerance
    val = int(round(val))
    return val

# Test for small N
for N in range(2,6):
    start = time.time()
    val = solve_max(N)
    print(N, val, "time", time.time()-start)

def generate_constraints(N):
    # returns list of tuples (indices list, rhs) for sum <= 3
    # index mapping: (i,j) -> i*N + j
    constraints = []
    # horizontal bases
    for y1 in range(N):
        for y2 in range(y1+1, N):
            dy = y2 - y1
            # choose a,b top row columns (order matters? a<b)
            for a in range(N):
                for b in range(a+1, N):
                    # need c,d bottom row such that |a-c| = |b-d|
                    # possibilities: c = a + t, d = b + t with t integer (could be negative) and within range
                    # also could be c = a - t, d = b - t (same as t negative)
                    # So we can iterate over t such that both c,d in [0,N-1]
                    for t in range(-N, N):
                        c = a + t
                        d = b + t
                        if 0 <= c < N and 0 <= d < N:
                            # ensure c<d? Not necessary; but bottom base should have c<d (order). Let's enforce c<d to avoid duplicate.
                            if c < d:
                                # add constraint for four points
                                idx = [y1*N + a, y1*N + b, y2*N + c, y2*N + d]
                                constraints.append(idx)
    # vertical bases
    for x1 in range(N):
        for x2 in range(x1+1, N):
            dx = x2 - x1
            for a in range(N):
                for b in range(a+1, N):
                    # a,b are y coordinates on left column x1? Actually points (x1,a), (x1,b) left base
                    # Need c,d on right column x2 such that |a-c| = |b-d|
                    for t in range(-N, N):
                        c = a + t
                        d = b + t
                        if 0 <= c < N and 0 <= d < N and c < d:
                            idx = [a*N + x1, b*N + x1, c*N + x2, d*N + x2]
                            constraints.append(idx)
    return constraints

def solve_max(N):
    constraints = generate_constraints(N)
    m = N*N
    # objective: maximize sum of x_i
    c = -np.ones(m)  # maximize => minimize -sum
    # Build A_ub matrix: each constraint sum <=3 => sum - 3 <= 0? Actually we need sum <= 3, so sum - 3 <= 0.
    # We'll create A_ub * x <= b_ub
    A = []
    b = []
    for idx in constraints:
        row = np.zeros(m, dtype=int)
        row[idx] = 1
        A.append(row)
        b.append(3)
    A = np.array(A, dtype=int)
    b = np.array(b, dtype=int)
    # variable bounds 0 <= x <=1, integer
    from scipy.optimize import milp, Bounds, LinearConstraint, OptimizeResult
    bounds = Bounds(0, 1)
    # integrality vector
    integrality = np.ones(m, dtype=int)
    lincon = LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, b)  # A x <= b
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=lincon, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("MILP failed")
        return None
    val = -res.fun  # because we minimized -sum
    # rounding due to tolerance
    val = int(round(val))
    return val

# Test for small N
for N in range(2,6):
    start = time.time()
    val = solve_max(N)
    print(N, val, "time", time.time()-start)

import itertools

def find_perm(N):
    for perm in itertools.permutations(range(1,N+1)):
        if any(perm[i]==i+1 for i in range(N)):
            continue
        diffs = {abs(perm[i]-(i+1)) for i in range(N)}
        if diffs == set(range(1,N)):
            return perm
    return None

for N in range(2,9):
    p=find_perm(N)
    print(N, p)

[CODE] (turn 5):
def find_perm_distinct(N):
    # backtrack
    used = [False]*(N)  # distances 1..N-1
    perm = [0]*N
    def backtrack(i):
        if i==N:
            return True
        for val in range(1,N+1):
            if val in perm[:i]:
                continue
            diff = abs(val-(i+1))
            if diff==0 or used[diff]:
                continue
            used[diff]=True
            perm[i]=val
            if backtrack(i+1):
                return True
            used[diff]=False
            perm[i]=0
        return False
    if backtrack(0):
        return tuple(perm)
    else:
        return None

for N in range(2,13):
    p=find_perm_distinct(N)
    print(N, p)

def find_perm_distinct(N):
    # backtrack
    used = [False]*(N)  # distances 1..N-1
    perm = [0]*N
    def backtrack(i):
        if i==N:
            return True
        for val in range(1,N+1):
            if val in perm[:i]:
                continue
            diff = abs(val-(i+1))
            if diff==0 or used[diff]:
                continue
            used[diff]=True
            perm[i]=val
            if backtrack(i+1):
                return True
            used[diff]=False
            perm[i]=0
        return False
    if backtrack(0):
        return tuple(perm)
    else:
        return None

[CODE] (turn 6):
import itertools

def find_config(N):
    rows = list(range(1,N+1))
    for r0 in rows:
        # rows with extra point: all except r0
        rows2 = [i for i in rows if i!=r0]
        cols2 = [j for j in rows if j!=r0]
        # need bijection g: rows2 -> cols2, with g(i)!=i (no fixed points)
        for perm in itertools.permutations(cols2):
            ok=True
            diffs=set()
            for i, j in zip(rows2, perm):
                if i==j:
                    ok=False
                    break
                d=abs(j-i)
                if d==0 or d in diffs:
                    ok=False
                    break
                diffs.add(d)
            if not ok:
                continue
            if diffs==set(range(1,N)):
                # found
                return r0, dict(zip(rows2, perm))
    return None

for N in range(2,9):
    res=find_config(N)
    print(N, res)

[CODE] (turn 2):
import itertools

def max_points(N):
    points = [(x,y) for x in range(1,N+1) for y in range(1,N+1)]
    maxM = 0
    best = None
    # brute force all subsets up to maybe N=3 (9 points)
    for r in range(len(points)+1):
        for subset in itertools.combinations(points, r):
            # quick prune if size <= maxM
            if r <= maxM:
                continue
            # check condition
            ok = True
            # Build dict rows->list cols, cols->list rows
            rows = {}
            cols = {}
            for x,y in subset:
                rows.setdefault(y, []).append(x)  # note: y is row? Actually y coordinate is vertical, but base parallel to x-axis uses same y.
                cols.setdefault(x, []).append(y)
            # check rows
            for y, xs in rows.items():
                if len(xs) >= 2:
                    # compute all unordered sums
                    sums = {}
                    for a,b in itertools.combinations(xs,2):
                        s = a+b
                        if s in sums:
                            ok=False
                            break
                        sums[s]=1
                if not ok:
                    break
            if not ok:
                continue
            # check columns
            for x, ys in cols.items():
                if len(ys) >= 2:
                    sums = {}
                    for a,b in itertools.combinations(ys,2):
                        s = a+b
                        if s in sums:
                            ok=False
                            break
                        sums[s]=1
                if not ok:
                    break
            if not ok:
                continue
            # also need to ensure no two rows share a sum, not just within a row
            # Actually condition: among selected points, no four points can form isosceles trapezoid with base parallel to axes.
            # This means for any two rows y1!=y2, there should not exist a,b in row y1 and c,d in row y2 with a+b = c+d.
            # So we need to check cross rows as well.
            # Let's compute for each row its set of sums, then ensure sets are disjoint across rows.
            row_sums = {}
            for y, xs in rows.items():
                sset = set()
                for a,b in itertools.combinations(xs,2):
                    sset.add(a+b)
                row_sums[y]=sset
            # check disjointness
            rows_list = list(row_sums.items())
            for i in range(len(rows_list)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(rows_list)):
                    if row_sums[rows_list[i][0]] & row_sums[rows_list[j][0]]:
                        ok=False
                        break
                if not ok:
                    break
            if not ok:
                continue
            # similarly for columns
            col_sums = {}
            for x, ys in cols.items():
                sset = set()
                for a,b in itertools.combinations(ys,2):
                    sset.add(a+b)
                col_sums[x]=sset
            cols_list = list(col_sums.items())
            for i in range(len(cols_list)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(cols_list)):
                    if col_sums[cols_list[i][0]] & col_sums[cols_list[j][0]]:
                        ok=False
                        break
                if not ok:
                    break
            if not ok:
                continue
            # if passes all checks
            maxM = r
            best = subset
    return maxM, best

print(max_points(3))

                        ok=False
                        break

                    break

[CODE] (turn 5):
def violates(selected_points, N):
    # selected_points: set of (x,y) with 0-indexed coordinates
    # check horizontal trapezoid condition
    # group by y
    rows = {}
    for x,y in selected_points:
        rows.setdefault(y, []).append(x)
    # for each pair of rows
    for y1 in rows:
        xs1 = rows[y1]
        if len(xs1) < 2:
            continue
        # compute sums of pairs in row y1
        sums1 = {}
        for a,b in itertools.combinations(xs1,2):
            s = a+b
            sums1.setdefault(s, []).append((a,b))
        for y2 in rows:
            if y2 <= y1:
                continue
            xs2 = rows[y2]
            if len(xs2) < 2:
                continue
            sums2 = {}
            for c,d in itertools.combinations(xs2,2):
                s = c+d
                sums2.setdefault(s, []).append((c,d))
            # check intersection of sums
            for s in sums1:
                if s in sums2:
                    # found same sum => isosceles trapezoid
                    return True
    # vertical condition
    cols = {}
    for x,y in selected_points:
        cols.setdefault(x, []).append(y)
    for x1 in cols:
        ys1 = cols[x1]
        if len(ys1) < 2:
            continue
        sums1 = {}
        for a,b in itertools.combinations(ys1,2):
            s = a+b
            sums1.setdefault(s, []).append((a,b))
        for x2 in cols:
            if x2 <= x1:
                continue
            ys2 = cols[x2]
            if len(ys2) < 2:
                continue
            sums2 = {}
            for c,d in itertools.combinations(ys2,2):
                s = c+d
                sums2.setdefault(s, []).append((c,d))
            for s in sums1:
                if s in sums2:
                    return True
    return False

def max_selected(N):
    # brute force for N small
    points = [(i,j) for i in range(N) for j in range(N)]
    best = 0
    best_set = None
    total = 1 << (N*N)
    # iterate all subsets (maybe huge)
    # We'll use BFS incremental search: start from largest possible size and test combos of that size.
    # Use itertools.combinations of points of size k.
    for k in range(N*N, -1, -1):
        # iterate combos of size k
        for combo in itertools.combinations(points, k):
            if not violates(set(combo), N):
                return k, set(combo)
    return 0, set()

[CODE] (turn 8):
def solve_N(N):
    # variable counts
    num_points = N*N
    sum_range = list(range(0, 2*(N-1)+1))  # sums of indices (0-indexed). Actually i+j ranges 0..2(N-1)
    # but we need sums from 0 to 2(N-1)
    S = sum_range
    num_u = N * len(S)
    num_v = N * len(S)
    total_vars = num_points + num_u + num_v
    
    # objective: maximize sum of point vars
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    c[:num_points] = -1.0  # milp minimizes, so negative for max
    
    # variable bounds: binary 0/1
    lb = np.zeros(total_vars)
    ub = np.ones(total_vars)
    
    # constraints list
    A_rows = []
    b_rows = []
    sense = []  # we will use LinearConstraint with lb=ub=some value? Actually LinearConstraint can define lower and upper bounds.
    # We'll collect constraints as (coeffs, lb, ub)
    # We'll accumulate in lists and later combine.
    constraints = []
    
    # Helper to get variable index
    def idx_point(i,j):
        return i*N + j  # i row (y), j column (x) maybe; but we treat i as row index (y), j as column (x)
    def idx_u(row, s):
        # row 0..N-1, s index in S list
        return num_points + row*len(S) + S.index(s)
    def idx_v(col, s):
        return num_points + num_u + col*len(S) + S.index(s)
    
    # Build constraints for each row and sum
    for row in range(N):
        for s in S:
            # get list of column pairs (c1,c2) with c1<c2 and c1+c2 == s
            pairs = [(c1,c2) for c1 in range(N) for c2 in range(c1+1,N) if c1 + c2 == s]
            if not pairs:
                # then u must be 0 (no pairs possible)
                # u <= 0 and u >= 0
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            # lower bound constraints: u >= x_i + x_j -1
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = -1  # move to left side: -u <= -(x_i + x_j -1) ??? Let's formulate as u - (x_i + x_j -1) >= 0
                # We'll use LinearConstraint with lower bound 0
                coeff[idx_point(row,c1)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c2)] = 1
                # inequality: u - x_i - x_j + 1 >= 0
                # i.e., coeff @ vars >= 0
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, np.inf))
            # upper bound: u <= sum_{pairs} (x_i + x_j -1)
            # This is linear: u - sum (x_i + x_j -1) <= 0
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                coeff[idx_point(row,c1)] -= 1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c2)] -= 1
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] -= -1  # wait we need -(-1)?? Actually sum (x_i + x_j -1) = sum x_i + sum x_j - (#pairs)
                # Let's compute RHS: sum (x_i + x_j -1) = sum x_i over all pairs + sum x_j over all pairs - (#pairs)
                # That's messy. Simpler: we can enforce u <= sum_{pairs} (x_i + x_j -1) by:
                # u - sum_{pairs} (x_i + x_j -1) <= 0
                # which is u - sum_{pairs} x_i - sum_{pairs} x_j + (#pairs) <= 0
                # So coefficient for u is 1, for each point variable we subtract its multiplicity (number of pairs it appears in), and constant term = #pairs.
                # LinearConstraint does not support constant term directly; we can move constant to bounds.
                pass

[CODE] (turn 8):
def solve_N(N):
    # variable counts
    num_points = N*N
    sum_range = list(range(0, 2*(N-1)+1))  # sums of indices (0-indexed). Actually i+j ranges 0..2(N-1)
    # but we need sums from 0 to 2(N-1)
    S = sum_range
    num_u = N * len(S)
    num_v = N * len(S)
    total_vars = num_points + num_u + num_v
    
    # objective: maximize sum of point vars
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    c[:num_points] = -1.0  # milp minimizes, so negative for max
    
    # variable bounds: binary 0/1
    lb = np.zeros(total_vars)
    ub = np.ones(total_vars)
    
    # constraints list
    A_rows = []
    b_rows = []
    sense = []  # we will use LinearConstraint with lb=ub=some value? Actually LinearConstraint can define lower and upper bounds.
    # We'll collect constraints as (coeffs, lb, ub)
    # We'll accumulate in lists and later combine.
    constraints = []

    def idx_point(i,j):
        return i*N + j  # i row (y), j column (x) maybe; but we treat i as row index (y), j as column (x)

        return num_points + row*len(S) + S.index(s)
    def idx_v(col, s):

                # We'll use LinearConstraint with lower bound 0
                coeff[idx_point(row,c1)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c2)] = 1
                # inequality: u - x_i - x_j + 1 >= 0
                # i.e., coeff @ vars >= 0
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, np.inf))

            # This is linear: u - sum (x_i + x_j -1) <= 0
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1

                coeff[idx_point(row,c2)] -= 1
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] -= -1  # wait we need -(-1)?? Actually sum (x_i + x_j -1) = sum x_i + sum x_j - (#pairs)

[CODE] (turn 9):
def solve_N(N, time_limit=30):
    # Setup MILP using scipy.optimize.milp
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    import numpy as np, itertools, math, time
    
    # index mapping
    num_points = N*N
    S = list(range(0, 2*(N-1)+1))
    lenS = len(S)
    num_u = N * lenS
    num_v = N * lenS
    total_vars = num_points + num_u + num_v
    
    def idx_point(row, col):
        return row*N + col
    def idx_u(row, s):
        return num_points + row*lenS + S.index(s)
    def idx_v(col, s):
        return num_points + num_u + col*lenS + S.index(s)
    
    # objective: maximize sum of point vars => minimize negative sum
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    c[:num_points] = -1.0
    
    # variable bounds binary
    lb = np.zeros(total_vars)
    ub = np.ones(total_vars)
    
    constraints = []
    
    # Row constraints
    for row in range(N):
        for s in S:
            # find pairs of columns (c1<c2) with c1+c2 = s
            pairs = [(c1,c2) for c1 in range(N) for c2 in range(c1+1,N) if c1 + c2 == s]
            if not pairs:
                # u must be 0
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            # lower bound constraints: u - x_c1 - x_c2 >= -1
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c1)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c2)] = -1
                # lower bound -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
            # upper bound: u - sum_{pairs} (x_i + x_j) <= -len(pairs)
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
            # collect columns involved
            cols_involved = set()
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                cols_involved.add(c1)
                cols_involved.add(c2)
            for c in cols_involved:
                coeff[idx_point(row,c)] = -1
            # upper bound = -len(pairs)
            constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, -len(pairs)))
    
    # Column constraints (vertical)
    for col in range(N):
        for s in S:
            # pairs of rows (r1<r2) with r1+r2 = s
            pairs = [(r1,r2) for r1 in range(N) for r2 in range(r1+1,N) if r1 + r2 == s]
            if not pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(r1,col)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(r2,col)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
            # upper bound
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
            rows_involved = set()
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                rows_involved.add(r1)
                rows_involved.add(r2)
            for r in rows_involved:
                coeff[idx_point(r,col)] = -1
            constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, -len(pairs)))
    
    # Cross-row constraints: for each sum s, for each pair of rows y1<y2: u_{y1,s}+u_{y2,s} <= 1
    for s in S:
        for y1 in range(N):
            for y2 in range(y1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(y1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_u(y2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    
    # Cross-column constraints: for each sum s, for each pair of cols x1<x2: v_{x1,s}+v_{x2,s} <= 1
    for s in S:
        for x1 in range(N):
            for x2 in range(x1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(x1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_v(x2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    
    # Build bounds object
    bounds = Bounds(lb, ub)
    
    # Solve MILP
    start = time.time()
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=np.arange(total_vars), options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': time_limit})
    elapsed = time.time() - start
    print("status:", res.success, "obj:", -res.fun if res.success else None, "time", elapsed)
    if not res.success:
        return None
    # retrieve solution
    x = res.x
    selected = set()
    for row in range(N):
        for col in range(N):
            if x[idx_point(row,col)] > 0.5:
                selected.add((col,row))  # (x,y) with x col, y row
    return selected

# Test for N=4, compare with brute force optimum 8
sel4 = solve_N(4, time_limit=10)
print(len(sel4))

                coeff[idx_point(r2,col)] = -1

            # upper bound
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
            rows_involved = set()

                rows_involved.add(r1)
                rows_involved.add(r2)
            for r in rows_involved:

Mixed-integer linear programming

    Solves problems of the following form:

    .. math::

        \min_x \ & c^T x \\
        \mbox{such that} \ & b_l \leq A x \leq b_u,\\
        & l \leq x \leq u, \\
        & x_i \in \mathbb{Z}, i \in X_i

    where :math:`x` is a vector of decision variables;
    :math:`c`, :math:`b_l`, :math:`b_u`, :math:`l`, and :math:`u` are vectors;
    :math:`A` is a matrix, and :math:`X_i` is the set of indices of
    decision variables that must be integral. (

    minimize::

        c @ x

    such that::

        b_l <= A @ x <= b_u
        l <= x <= u
        Specified elements of x must be integers

    By default, ``l = 0`` and ``u = np.inf`` unless specified with
    ``bounds``.

    Parameters
    ----------
    c : 1D dense array_like
        The coefficients of the linear objective

[OUTPUT] (turn 15):
Help on function milp in module scipy.optimize._milp:

milp(c, *, integrality=None, bounds=None, constraints=None, options=None)
    Mixed-integer linear programming

    Solves problems of the following form:

    .. math::

        \min_x \ & c^T x \\
        \mbox{such that} \ & b_l \leq A x \leq b_u,\\
        & l \leq x \leq u, \\
        & x_i \in \mathbb{Z}, i \in X_i

    where :math:`x` is a vector of decision variables;
    :math:`c`, :math:`b_l`, :math:`b_u`, :math:`l`, and :math:`u` are vectors;
    :math:`A` is a matrix, and :math:`X_i` is the set of indices of
    decision variables that must be integral. (In this context, a
    variable that can assume only integer values is said to be "integral";
    it has an "integrality" constraint.)

    Alternatively, that's:

    minimize::

        c @ x

    such that::

        b_l <= A @ x <= b_u
        l <= x <= u
        Specified elements of x must be integers

    By default, ``l = 0`` and ``u = np.inf`` unless specified with
    ``bounds``.

    Parameters
    ----------
    c : 1D dense array_like
        The coefficients of the linear objective function to be minimized.
        `c` is converted to a double precision array before the problem is
        solved.
    integrality : 1D dense array_like, optional
        Indicates the type of integrality constraint on each decision variable.

        ``0`` : Continuous variable; no integrality constraint.

        ``1`` : Integer variable; decision variable must be an integer
        within `bounds`.

... (201 lines total)

[CODE] (turn 16):
def solve_N(N, time_limit=30):
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    import numpy as np, itertools, math, time
    
    num_points = N*N
    S = list(range(0, 2*(N-1)+1))
    lenS = len(S)
    num_u = N * lenS
    num_v = N * lenS
    total_vars = num_points + num_u + num_v
    
    def idx_point(row, col):
        return row*N + col
    def idx_u(row, s):
        return num_points + row*lenS + S.index(s)
    def idx_v(col, s):
        return num_points + num_u + col*lenS + S.index(s)
    
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    c[:num_points] = -1.0
    
    # bounds binary
    lb = np.zeros(total_vars)
    ub = np.ones(total_vars)
    bounds = Bounds(lb, ub)
    
    constraints = []
    
    # Row constraints
    for row in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs = [(c1,c2) for c1 in range(N) for c2 in range(c1+1,N) if c1 + c2 == s]
            if not pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            # lower bound constraints: u - x_c1 - x_c2 >= -1
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c1)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c2)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
            # upper bound: u - sum_{involved cols} x_c <= -len(pairs)
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
            cols_involved = set()
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                cols_involved.add(c1)
                cols_involved.add(c2)
            for c in cols_involved:
                coeff[idx_point(row,c)] = -1
            constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, -len(pairs)))
    
    # Column constraints
    for col in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs = [(r1,r2) for r1 in range(N) for r2 in range(r1+1,N) if r1 + r2 == s]
            if not pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(r1,col)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(r2,col)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
            rows_involved = set()
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                rows_involved.add(r1)
                rows_involved.add(r2)
            for r in rows_involved:
                coeff[idx_point(r,col)] = -1
            constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, -len(pairs)))
    
    # Cross-row constraints
    for s in S:
        for y1 in range(N):
            for y2 in range(y1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(y1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_u(y2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    
    # Cross-column constraints
    for s in S:
        for x1 in range(N):
            for x2 in range(x1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(x1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_v(x2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    
    integrality = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)  # all integer (binary)
    
    start = time.time()
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': time_limit})
    elapsed = time.time() - start
    print("MILP status:", res.success, "obj:", -res.fun if res.success else None, "time", elapsed)
    if not res.success:
        return None
    x = res.x
    selected = set()
    for row in range(N):
        for col in range(N):
            if x[idx_point(row,col)] > 0.5:
                selected.add((col,row))
    return selected

sel4 = solve_N(4, time_limit=10)
print("selected count", len(sel4))

def solve_N(N, time_limit=30):
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    import numpy as np, itertools, math, time
    
    num_points = N*N

    num_v = N * lenS

    def idx_point(row, col):
        return row*N + col
    def idx_u(row, s):
        return num_points + row*lenS + S.index(s)
    def idx_v(col, s):
        return num_points + num_u + col*lenS + S.index(s)

            if not pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            # lower bound constraints: u - x_c1 - x_c2 >= -1

            if not pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(r1,col)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(r2,col)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
            rows_involved = set()
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                rows_involved.add(r1)
                rows_involved.add(r2)
            for r in rows_involved:
                coeff[idx_point(r,col)] = -1
            constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, -len(pairs)))
    
    # Cross-row constraints
    for s in S:
        for y1 in range(N):
            for y2 in range(y1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(y1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_u(y2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    
    # Cross-column constraints
    for s in S:
        for x1 in range(N):
            for x2 in range(x1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(x1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_v(x2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    
    integrality = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)  # all integer (binary)
    
    start = time.time()
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': time_limit})
    elapsed = time.time() - start
    print("MILP status:", res.success, "obj:", -res.fun if res.success else None, "time", elapsed)
    if not res.success:
        return None
    x = res.x
    selected = set()
    for row in range(N):
        for col in range(N):
            if x[idx_point(row,col)] > 0.5:
                selected.add((col,row))
    return selected

def test_milp(N):

    S = list(range(0, 2*(N-1)+1))
    lenS = len(S)
    total_vars = num_points + N*lenS + N*lenS
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    bounds = Bounds(np.zeros(total_vars), np.ones(total_vars))
    integrality = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)
    # no constraints
    res = milp(c=c, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality)
    print("res", res.success)

def solve_N_debug(N, time_limit=10):
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    import numpy as np, itertools, math, time
    num_points = N*N
    S = list(range(0, 2*(N-1)+1))
    lenS = len(S)
    num_u = N * lenS
    num_v = N * lenS
    total_vars = num_points + num_u + num_v
    print("total_vars", total_vars)
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    c[:num_points] = -1.0
    lb = np.zeros(total_vars)
    ub = np.ones(total_vars)
    bounds = Bounds(lb, ub)
    constraints = []
    def idx_point(row, col):
        return row*N + col
    def idx_u(row, s):
        return num_points + row*lenS + S.index(s)
    def idx_v(col, s):
        return num_points + num_u + col*lenS + S.index(s)
    # Row constraints
    for row in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs = [(c1,c2) for c1 in range(N) for c2 in range(c1+1,N) if c1 + c2 == s]
            if not pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c1)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c2)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
            cols_involved = set()
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                cols_involved.add(c1)
                cols_involved.add(c2)
            for c in cols_involved:
                coeff[idx_point(row,c)] = -1
            constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, -len(pairs)))
    # Column constraints (similar)
    for col in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs = [(r1,r2) for r1 in range(N) for r2 in range(r1+1,N) if r1 + r2 == s]
            if not pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(r1,col)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(r2,col)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
            rows_involved = set()
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                rows_involved.add(r1)
                rows_involved.add(r2)
            for r in rows_involved:
                coeff[idx_point(r,col)] = -1
            constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, -len(pairs)))
    # Cross constraints
    for s in S:
        for y1 in range(N):
            for y2 in range(y1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(y1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_u(y2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    for s in S:
        for x1 in range(N):
            for x2 in range(x1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(x1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_v(x2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    print("num constraints", len(constraints))
    integrality = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)
    start = time.time()
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': time_limit})
    print("status", res.success)
    return res

def solve_N_debug(N, time_limit=10):
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    import numpy as np, itertools, math, time
    num_points = N*N
    S = list(range(0, 2*(N-1)+1))

    ub = np.ones(total_vars)
    bounds = Bounds(lb, ub)
    constraints = []
    def idx_point(row, col):
        return row*N + col
    def idx_u(row, s):
        return num_points + row*lenS + S.index(s)
    def idx_v(col, s):
        return num_points + num_u + col*lenS + S.index(s)

            if not pairs:

                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c1)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c2)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1

                cols_involved.add(c1)
                cols_involved.add(c2)
            for c in cols_involved:
                coeff[idx_point(row,c)] = -1
            constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, -len(pairs)))
    # Column constraints (similar)
    for col in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs = [(r1,r2) for r1 in range(N) for r2 in range(r1+1,N) if r1 + r2 == s]
            if not pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, 0, 0))
                continue
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(r1,col)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(r2,col)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
            coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
            coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
            rows_involved = set()
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                rows_involved.add(r1)
                rows_involved.add(r2)
            for r in rows_involved:
                coeff[idx_point(r,col)] = -1
            constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, -len(pairs)))
    # Cross constraints
    for s in S:
        for y1 in range(N):
            for y2 in range(y1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(y1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_u(y2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    for s in S:
        for x1 in range(N):
            for x2 in range(x1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(x1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_v(x2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    print("num constraints", len(constraints))
    integrality = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)
    start = time.time()
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': time_limit})
    print("status", res.success)
    return res

solve_N_debug(4, time_limit=5)

[CODE] (turn 27):
def solve_N_simple(N):
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    import numpy as np, itertools, math, time
    num_points=N*N
    S=list(range(0,2*(N-1)+1))
    lenS=len(S)
    total_vars=num_points+2*N*lenS
    c=np.zeros(total_vars); c[:num_points]=-1
    bounds=Bounds(np.zeros(total_vars), np.ones(total_vars))
    constraints=[]
    # just add simple constraints: each point <=1 (already bounds)
    # add cross-row constraints only for demonstration
    # Not adding any constraints else
    integrality=np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)
    res=milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality)
    print("solved", res.success, "obj", -res.fun)
solve_N_simple(3)

[CODE] (turn 28):
def solve_N_rows_only(N):
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    import numpy as np, itertools, math, time
    num_points=N*N
    S=list(range(0,2*(N-1)+1))
    lenS=len(S)
    total_vars=num_points+2*N*lenS
    c=np.zeros(total_vars); c[:num_points]=-1
    bounds=Bounds(np.zeros(total_vars), np.ones(total_vars))
    constraints=[]
    def idx_point(row,col):
        return row*N+col
    def idx_u(row,s):
        return num_points + row*lenS + S.index(s)
    # Row constraints
    for row in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs=[(c1,c2) for c1 in range(N) for c2 in range(c1+1,N) if c1+c2==s]
            if not pairs:
                coeff=np.zeros(total_vars); coeff[idx_u(row,s)]=1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff,0,0))
                continue
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                coeff=np.zeros(total_vars); coeff[idx_u(row,s)]=1; coeff[idx_point(row,c1)]=-1; coeff[idx_point(row,c2)]=-1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff,-1,np.inf))
            coeff=np.zeros(total_vars); coeff[idx_u(row,s)]=1
            cols=set()
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                cols.add(c1); cols.add(c2)
            for c in cols:
                coeff[idx_point(row,c)]=-1
            constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff,-np.inf,-len(pairs)))
    # cross-row constraints
    for s in S:
        for y1 in range(N):
            for y2 in range(y1+1,N):
                coeff=np.zeros(total_vars); coeff[idx_u(y1,s)]=1; coeff[idx_u(y2,s)]=1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff,-np.inf,1))
    integrality=np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)
    res=milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality)
    print("status", res.success, "obj", -res.fun)
solve_N_rows_only(4)

def solve_N_rows_only(N):
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    import numpy as np, itertools, math, time
    num_points=N*N
    S=list(range(0,2*(N-1)+1))
    lenS=len(S)
    total_vars=num_points+2*N*lenS
    c=np.zeros(total_vars); c[:num_points]=-1
    bounds=Bounds(np.zeros(total_vars), np.ones(total_vars))
    constraints=[]

        return row*N+col
    def idx_u(row,s):
        return num_points + row*lenS + S.index(s)
    # Row constraints
    for row in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs=[(c1,c2) for c1 in range(N) for c2 in range(c1+1,N) if c1+c2==s]
            if not pairs:
                coeff=np.zeros(total_vars); coeff[idx_u(row,s)]=1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff,0,0))
                continue
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                coeff=np.zeros(total_vars); coeff[idx_u(row,s)]=1; coeff[idx_point(row,c1)]=-1; coeff[idx_point(row,c2)]=-1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff,-1,np.inf))
            coeff=np.zeros(total_vars); coeff[idx_u(row,s)]=1
            cols=set()

                cols.add(c1); cols.add(c2)
            for c in cols:
                coeff[idx_point(row,c)]=-1

    # cross-row constraints
    for s in S:
        for y1 in range(N):
            for y2 in range(y1+1,N):
                coeff=np.zeros(total_vars); coeff[idx_u(y1,s)]=1; coeff[idx_u(y2,s)]=1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff,-np.inf,1))
    integrality=np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)
    res=milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality)

[CODE] (turn 30):
def solve_N_simple_constraints(N, time_limit=10):
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    import numpy as np, itertools, time
    num_points = N*N
    S = list(range(0, 2*(N-1)+1))
    lenS = len(S)
    total_vars = num_points + 2*N*lenS
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    c[:num_points] = -1.0
    bounds = Bounds(np.zeros(total_vars), np.ones(total_vars))
    constraints = []
    def idx_point(row,col):
        return row*N + col
    def idx_u(row,s):
        return num_points + row*lenS + S.index(s)
    def idx_v(col,s):
        return num_points + N*lenS + col*lenS + S.index(s)
    # Row lower bound constraints
    for row in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs = [(c1,c2) for c1 in range(N) for c2 in range(c1+1,N) if c1 + c2 == s]
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c1)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c2)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
    # Column lower bound constraints
    for col in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs = [(r1,r2) for r1 in range(N) for r2 in range(r1+1,N) if r1 + r2 == s]
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(r1,col)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(r2,col)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
    # Cross-row constraints
    for s in S:
        for y1 in range(N):
            for y2 in range(y1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(y1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_u(y2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    # Cross-col constraints
    for s in S:
        for x1 in range(N):
            for x2 in range(x1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(x1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_v(x2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    integrality = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': time_limit})
    print("status", res.success, "obj", -res.fun if res.success else None)
    if not res.success:
        return None
    x = res.x
    selected = set()
    for row in range(N):
        for col in range(N):
            if x[idx_point(row,col)] > 0.5:
                selected.add((col,row))
    return selected

sel4 = solve_N_simple_constraints(4, time_limit=10)
print(len(sel4))

def solve_N_simple_constraints(N, time_limit=10):
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    import numpy as np, itertools, time
    num_points = N*N
    S = list(range(0, 2*(N-1)+1))
    lenS = len(S)
    total_vars = num_points + 2*N*lenS
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    c[:num_points] = -1.0
    bounds = Bounds(np.zeros(total_vars), np.ones(total_vars))
    constraints = []
    def idx_point(row,col):
        return row*N + col
    def idx_u(row,s):
        return num_points + row*lenS + S.index(s)
    def idx_v(col,s):
        return num_points + N*lenS + col*lenS + S.index(s)
    # Row lower bound constraints
    for row in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs = [(c1,c2) for c1 in range(N) for c2 in range(c1+1,N) if c1 + c2 == s]
            for (c1,c2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(row,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c1)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(row,c2)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
    # Column lower bound constraints
    for col in range(N):
        for s in S:
            pairs = [(r1,r2) for r1 in range(N) for r2 in range(r1+1,N) if r1 + r2 == s]
            for (r1,r2) in pairs:
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(col,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_point(r1,col)] = -1
                coeff[idx_point(r2,col)] = -1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -1, np.inf))
    # Cross-row constraints
    for s in S:
        for y1 in range(N):
            for y2 in range(y1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_u(y1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_u(y2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    # Cross-col constraints
    for s in S:
        for x1 in range(N):
            for x2 in range(x1+1,N):
                coeff = np.zeros(total_vars)
                coeff[idx_v(x1,s)] = 1
                coeff[idx_v(x2,s)] = 1
                constraints.append(LinearConstraint(coeff, -np.inf, 1))
    integrality = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': time_limit})
    print("status", res.success, "obj", -res.fun if res.success else None)
    if not res.success:
        return None
    x = res.x
    selected = set()
    for row in range(N):
        for col in range(N):
            if x[idx_point(row,col)] > 0.5:
                selected.add((col,row))
    return selected

[CODE] (turn 1):
def sigma(N):
    perm = {}
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        val = (2*i) % N
        if val == 0:
            val = N
        perm[i]=val
    return perm

def check(N):
    perm = sigma(N)
    sums = {}
    diffs = {}
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        s = i+perm[i]
        d = i-perm[i]
        if s in sums:
            return False, "sum duplicate"
        sums[s]=i
        if d in diffs:
            return False, "diff duplicate"
        diffs[d]=i
    return True, perm

for N in [5,7,9,11]:
    ok, info = check(N)
    print(N, ok)

import itertools
import math
from functools import lru_cache

def max_points(N):
    # Use MILP via pulp? Not available. Use brute force enumeration for small N.
    # We'll iterate over all subsets of grid points? That's 2^(N^2) huge.
    # Instead we can search over degree sequences and use Gale-Ryser condition.
    # For small N we can brute force all 0/1 matrices using itertools product.
    maxE=0
    best=None
    cells = [(i,j) for i in range(N) for j in range(N)]
    # brute force for N up to 4 maybe
    for mask in range(1<< (N*N)):
        # compute quickly? Not feasible for N>3
        pass

import itertools
import math
from functools import lru_cache

def max_points(N):
    # Use MILP via pulp? Not available. Use brute force enumeration for small N.
    # We'll iterate over all subsets of grid points? That's 2^(N^2) huge.
    # Instead we can search over degree sequences and use Gale-Ryser condition.
    # For small N we can brute force all 0/1 matrices using itertools product.
    maxE=0
    best=None
    cells = [(i,j) for i in range(N) for j in range(N)]
    # brute force for N up to 4 maybe
    for mask in range(1<< (N*N)):
        # compute quickly? Not feasible for N>3
        pass

[CODE] (turn 6):
def max_points_bruteforce(N):
    # backtracking row by row
    maxE=0
    best=None
    # precompute all unordered pairs of columns for each possible subset of columns
    from itertools import combinations, product

    # Represent each row by a tuple of selected columns (sorted)
    # We'll generate possible subsets per row (including empty)
    all_subsets = [tuple()] + [tuple([c]) for c in range(N)] + [tuple(comb) for comb in combinations(range(N),2)] + [tuple(comb) for comb in combinations(range(N),3)]
    # but we may need up to N columns per row; but for small N we can limit to size <=3 maybe.
    # We'll generate all subsets up to size 3 for now.
    subsets_by_size = {}
    for s in all_subsets:
        subsets_by_size.setdefault(len(s), []).append(s)

    # We'll recursively assign rows
    used_sum = set()
    used_diff = set()
    col_counts = [0]*N

    def recurse(row, currentE):
        nonlocal maxE, best
        if row == N:
            if currentE > maxE:
                maxE = currentE
                best = list(col_counts)
            return
        # prune: maximum possible remaining points = currentE + (N-row)*N (if all cells)
        # but we have constraints, not helpful.
        # Try each possible subset for this row
        for subset in subsets_by_size[0] + subsets_by_size[1] + subsets_by_size[2] + subsets_by_size[3]:
            # check column degree constraint? Not needed yet.
            # compute new sums/diffs for this row
            ok=True
            new_sums=[]
            new_diffs=[]
            # for each unordered pair in subset
            for a,b in combinations(subset,2):
                s = a+b
                d = a-b
                if s in used_sum or d in used_diff:
                    ok=False
                    break
                new_sums.append(s)
                new_diffs.append(d)
            if not ok:
                continue
            # also need column constraints later; for now ignore.
            # update
            for s in new_sums: used_sum.add(s)
            for d in new_diffs: used_diff.add(d)
            # update column counts
            for c in subset:
                col_counts[c]+=1
            recurse(row+1, currentE+len(subset))
            # backtrack
            for s in new_sums: used_sum.remove(s)
            for d in new_diffs: used_diff.remove(d)
            for c in subset:
                col_counts[c]-=1

    recurse(0,0)
    return maxE, best

for N in range(2,7):
    maxE,_ = max_points_bruteforce(N)
    print(N, maxE)

[CODE] (turn 7):
def max_points_bruteforce(N, max_row_size=2):
    from itertools import combinations
    subsets = [tuple()] + [tuple([c]) for c in range(N)]
    for k in range(2, max_row_size+1):
        for comb in combinations(range(N), k):
            subsets.append(comb)
    # sort maybe
    maxE=0
    best_rows=None
    used_sum=set()
    used_diff=set()
    col_counts=[0]*N

    def recurse(row, currentE, rows_selected):
        nonlocal maxE, best_rows
        if row==N:
            if currentE>maxE:
                maxE=currentE
                best_rows=rows_selected.copy()
            return
        # prune: maximum possible remaining points = currentE + (N-row)*max_row_size
        if currentE + (N-row)*max_row_size <= maxE:
            return
        for subset in subsets:
            # skip subsets larger than max_row_size
            if len(subset)>max_row_size:
                continue
            # check column degree not exceed something? Not needed for upper bound.
            # check sums/diffs
            ok=True
            new_sums=[]
            new_diffs=[]
            for a,b in combinations(subset,2):
                s=a+b
                d=a-b
                if s in used_sum or d in used_diff:
                    ok=False
                    break
                new_sums.append(s)
                new_diffs.append(d)
            if not ok:
                continue
            # update
            for s in new_sums: used_sum.add(s)
            for d in new_diffs: used_diff.add(d)
            for c in subset:
                col_counts[c]+=1
            rows_selected.append(subset)
            recurse(row+1, currentE+len(subset), rows_selected)
            rows_selected.pop()
            for s in new_sums: used_sum.remove(s)
            for d in new_diffs: used_diff.remove(d)
            for c in subset:
                col_counts[c]-=1

    recurse(0,0,[])
    return maxE, best_rows, col_counts

for N in range(2,9):
    maxE, rows, cols = max_points_bruteforce(N, max_row_size=2)
    print(N, maxE)

N=9973
import math
# solve quadratic inequality P^2 <= N*(4*N - 2 + P)
# Rearranged: P^2 - N*P - N*(4*N - 2) <= 0
a=1
b=-N
c= -N*(4*N - 2)
# roots of quadratic equation a P^2 + b P + c = 0
disc = b*b - 4*a*c
root1 = (-b - math.sqrt(disc)) / (2*a)
root2 = (-b + math.sqrt(disc)) / (2*a)
root1, root2

import math
# solve quadratic inequality P^2 <= N*(4*N - 2 + P)

a=1
b=-N
c= -N*(4*N - 2)
# roots of quadratic equation a P^2 + b P + c = 0
disc = b*b - 4*a*c
root1 = (-b - math.sqrt(disc)) / (2*a)
root2 = (-b + math.sqrt(disc)) / (2*a)
root1, root2

[CODE] (turn 10):
import itertools
import math
from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
import numpy as np

def max_points_milp(N):
    # variables x_{i,j} for i row index 0..N-1, j column 0..N-1
    n_vars = N*N
    # objective: maximize sum of x
    c = -np.ones(n_vars)  # milp minimizes, so negative
    # constraints: for each unordered pair of rows r1<r2, for each unordered pair of columns a<b, we need to avoid isosceles trapezoid.
    # We'll generate constraints of form x_{r1,a}+x_{r1,b}+x_{r2,c}+x_{r2,d} <= 3 if condition holds? Actually we need to forbid having all four =1.
    # So constraint: x1 + x2 + x3 + x4 <= 3.
    # We need to consider all combinations where a,b,c,d satisfy a+b = c+d or a-b = c-d.
    # We'll generate for each pair of rows and each unordered pair of columns (a,b) for row r1, and each unordered pair (c,d) for row r2 such that condition holds.
    rows = list(range(N))
    cols = list(range(N))
    A = []
    b = []
    # iterate rows
    for r1 in range(N):
        for r2 in range(r1+1,N):
            # generate all unordered pairs of columns for each row
            for a,bcol in itertools.combinations(cols,2):
                for c,dcol in itertools.combinations(cols,2):
                    # check condition
                    if (a + bcol == c + dcol) or (a - bcol == c - dcol):
                        # create constraint
                        coeff = np.zeros(n_vars)
                        idx1 = r1*N + a
                        idx2 = r1*N + bcol
                        idx3 = r2*N + c
                        idx4 = r2*N + dcol
                        coeff[idx1]=1
                        coeff[idx2]=1
                        coeff[idx3]=1
                        coeff[idx4]=1
                        A.append(coeff)
                        b.append(3)
    # Also need constraints for vertical bases (swap rows/cols). But our constraints already consider rows as base parallel to x-axis. For vertical bases, we need similar constraints with rows and columns swapped.
    # We'll add constraints for each pair of columns and rows analogous.
    for c1 in range(N):
        for c2 in range(c1+1,N):
            for a,brow in itertools.combinations(rows,2):
                for c,drow in itertools.combinations(rows,2):
                    if (a + brow == c + drow) or (a - brow == c - drow):
                        coeff = np.zeros(n_vars)
                        idx1 = a*N + c1
                        idx2 = brow*N + c1
                        idx3 = c*N + c2
                        idx4 = drow*N + c2
                        coeff[idx1]=1
                        coeff[idx2]=1
                        coeff[idx3]=1
                        coeff[idx4]=1
                        A.append(coeff)
                        b.append(3)
    # Convert to arrays
    A = np.array(A)
    b = np.array(b)
    # bounds for variables 0<=x<=1 integer
    bounds = Bounds(0,1)
    # integrality: all variables integer
    integrality = np.ones(n_vars)
    # Solve MILP
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, b), bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("MILP failed")
        return None
    x = np.round(res.x).astype(int)
    total = int(x.sum())
    return total

for N in range(2,6):
    print(N, max_points_milp(N))

import itertools
import math
from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
import numpy as np

def max_points_milp(N):
    # variables x_{i,j} for i row index 0..N-1, j column 0..N-1
    n_vars = N*N
    # objective: maximize sum of x
    c = -np.ones(n_vars)  # milp minimizes, so negative
    # constraints: for each unordered pair of rows r1<r2, for each unordered pair of columns a<b, we need to avoid isosceles trapezoid.
    # We'll generate constraints of form x_{r1,a}+x_{r1,b}+x_{r2,c}+x_{r2,d} <= 3 if condition holds? Actually we need to forbid having all four =1.
    # So constraint: x1 + x2 + x3 + x4 <= 3.
    # We need to consider all combinations where a,b,c,d satisfy a+b = c+d or a-b = c-d.
    # We'll generate for each pair of rows and each unordered pair of columns (a,b) for row r1, and each unordered pair (c,d) for row r2 such that condition holds.
    rows = list(range(N))
    cols = list(range(N))
    A = []
    b = []
    # iterate rows
    for r1 in range(N):
        for r2 in range(r1+1,N):
            # generate all unordered pairs of columns for each row
            for a,bcol in itertools.combinations(cols,2):
                for c,dcol in itertools.combinations(cols,2):
                    # check condition
                    if (a + bcol == c + dcol) or (a - bcol == c - dcol):
                        # create constraint
                        coeff = np.zeros(n_vars)
                        idx1 = r1*N + a
                        idx2 = r1*N + bcol
                        idx3 = r2*N + c
                        idx4 = r2*N + dcol
                        coeff[idx1]=1
                        coeff[idx2]=1
                        coeff[idx3]=1
                        coeff[idx4]=1
                        A.append(coeff)
                        b.append(3)
    # Also need constraints for vertical bases (swap rows/cols). But our constraints already consider rows as base parallel to x-axis. For vertical bases, we need similar constraints with rows and columns swapped.
    # We'll add constraints for each pair of columns and rows analogous.
    for c1 in range(N):
        for c2 in range(c1+1,N):
            for a,brow in itertools.combinations(rows,2):
                for c,drow in itertools.combinations(rows,2):
                    if (a + brow == c + drow) or (a - brow == c - drow):
                        coeff = np.zeros(n_vars)
                        idx1 = a*N + c1
                        idx2 = brow*N + c1
                        idx3 = c*N + c2
                        idx4 = drow*N + c2
                        coeff[idx1]=1
                        coeff[idx2]=1
                        coeff[idx3]=1
                        coeff[idx4]=1
                        A.append(coeff)
                        b.append(3)
    # Convert to arrays
    A = np.array(A)
    b = np.array(b)
    # bounds for variables 0<=x<=1 integer
    bounds = Bounds(0,1)
    # integrality: all variables integer
    integrality = np.ones(n_vars)
    # Solve MILP
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, b), bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("MILP failed")
        return None
    x = np.round(res.x).astype(int)
    total = int(x.sum())
    return total

for N in range(2,6):
    print(N, max_points_milp(N))

[CODE] (turn 11):
def max_points_milp(N):
    n_vars = N*N
    c = -np.ones(n_vars, dtype=int)  # minimize negative sum
    rows = list(range(N))
    cols = list(range(N))
    constraints = []
    lbs = []
    ubs = []
    # horizontal bases constraints
    for r1 in range(N):
        for r2 in range(r1+1,N):
            for a,bcol in itertools.combinations(cols,2):
                for ccol,dcol in itertools.combinations(cols,2):
                    if (a + bcol == ccol + dcol) or (a - bcol == ccol - dcol):
                        coeff = np.zeros(n_vars, dtype=int)
                        coeff[r1*N + a] = 1
                        coeff[r1*N + bcol] = 1
                        coeff[r2*N + ccol] = 1
                        coeff[r2*N + dcol] = 1
                        constraints.append(coeff)
                        lbs.append(-np.inf)
                        ubs.append(3)
    # vertical bases constraints
    for c1 in range(N):
        for c2 in range(c1+1,N):
            for a,brow in itertools.combinations(rows,2):
                for c,drow in itertools.combinations(rows,2):
                    if (a + brow == c + drow) or (a - brow == c - drow):
                        coeff = np.zeros(n_vars, dtype=int)
                        coeff[a*N + c1] = 1
                        coeff[brow*N + c1] = 1
                        coeff[c*N + c2] = 1
                        coeff[drow*N + c2] = 1
                        constraints.append(coeff)
                        lbs.append(-np.inf)
                        ubs.append(3)
    if not constraints:
        A = None
        lb = None
        ub = None
    else:
        A = np.vstack(constraints)
        lb = np.array(lbs)
        ub = np.array(ubs)
    bounds = Bounds(0,1)
    integrality = np.ones(n_vars, dtype=int)
    # Solve
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=LinearConstraint(A, lb, ub), bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("MILP failed", res.message)
        return None
    x = np.round(res.x).astype(int)
    return int(x.sum())

for N in range(2,5):
    print(N, max_points_milp(N))

def max_points_milp(N):
    n_vars = N*N
    c = -np.ones(n_vars, dtype=float)  # objective coefficients
    rows = list(range(N))
    cols = list(range(N))
    constraints = []
    lbs = []
    ubs = []
    for r1 in range(N):
        for r2 in range(r1+1,N):
            for a,bcol in itertools.combinations(cols,2):
                for ccol,dcol in itertools.combinations(cols,2):
                    if (a + bcol == ccol + dcol) or (a - bcol == ccol - dcol):
                        coeff = np.zeros(n_vars, dtype=float)
                        coeff[r1*N + a] = 1
                        coeff[r1*N + bcol] = 1
                        coeff[r2*N + ccol] = 1
                        coeff[r2*N + dcol] = 1
                        constraints.append(coeff)
                        lbs.append(-np.inf)
                        ubs.append(3)
    for c1 in range(N):
        for c2 in range(c1+1,N):
            for a,brow in itertools.combinations(rows,2):
                for c,drow in itertools.combinations(rows,2):
                    if (a + brow == c + drow) or (a - brow == c - drow):
                        coeff = np.zeros(n_vars, dtype=float)
                        coeff[a*N + c1] = 1
                        coeff[brow*N + c1] = 1
                        coeff[c*N + c2] = 1
                        coeff[drow*N + c2] = 1
                        constraints.append(coeff)
                        lbs.append(-np.inf)
                        ubs.append(3)
    if constraints:
        A = np.vstack(constraints)
        lb = np.array(lbs)
        ub = np.array(ubs)
        lincon = LinearConstraint(A, lb, ub)
    else:
        lincon = None
    bounds = Bounds(0,1)
    integrality = np.ones(n_vars, dtype=int)
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=lincon, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("MILP failed", res.message)
        return None
    x = np.round(res.x).astype(int)
    return int(x.sum())

    print(N, max_points_milp(N))
