            else:
                row_sum_used[s] = prev
        for s, prev in saved_col.items():
            if prev is None:
                del col_sum_used[s]
            else:
                col_sum_used[s] = prev
    
    selected = set()
    backtrack(0,0)
    return best, solution

[CODE] (turn 7):
def construct(n):
    S_max = 2*n - 3
    rows_k3 = (n-3)//2  # floor
    rows_k2 = n - rows_k3
    # assign groups of three sums starting from odd 3
    group_starts = list(range(3, 2*n, 4))[:rows_k3]
    used_sums = set()
    rows = {}
    row_idx = 1
    for s in group_starts:
        a = (s-1)//2
        rows[row_idx] = [a, a+1, a+2]
        used_sums.update([s, s+1, s+2])
        row_idx += 1
    # remaining sums
    remaining_sums = [x for x in range(3, 2*n) if x not in used_sums]
    # assign each to a row with two points
    for r in remaining_sums:
        # choose x = max(1, r - n)
        x = max(1, r - n)
        y = r - x
        if x==y:
            # need distinct, adjust
            x+=1
            y-=1
        rows[row_idx] = [x, y]
        row_idx += 1
    # total points
    total = sum(len(cols) for cols in rows.values())
    return rows, total

rows,total = construct(5)
total, rows

[CODE] (turn 7):
def construct(n):
    S_max = 2*n - 3
    rows_k3 = (n-3)//2  # floor
    rows_k2 = n - rows_k3
    # assign groups of three sums starting from odd 3
    group_starts = list(range(3, 2*n, 4))[:rows_k3]
    used_sums = set()
    rows = {}
    row_idx = 1
    for s in group_starts:
        a = (s-1)//2
        rows[row_idx] = [a, a+1, a+2]
        used_sums.update([s, s+1, s+2])
        row_idx += 1
    # remaining sums
    remaining_sums = [x for x in range(3, 2*n) if x not in used_sums]
    # assign each to a row with two points
    for r in remaining_sums:
        # choose x = max(1, r - n)
        x = max(1, r - n)
        y = r - x
        if x==y:
            # need distinct, adjust
            x+=1
            y-=1
        rows[row_idx] = [x, y]
        row_idx += 1
    # total points
    total = sum(len(cols) for cols in rows.values())
    return rows, total

[CODE] (turn 8):
def has_forbidden(rows):
    # rows dict y->list of x
    ys = list(rows.keys())
    for i in range(len(ys)):
        for j in range(i+1,len(ys)):
            y1, y2 = ys[i], ys[j]
            xs1 = rows[y1]
            xs2 = rows[y2]
            # consider all unordered pairs from each row
            for a_idx in range(len(xs1)):
                for b_idx in range(a_idx+1,len(xs1)):
                    a = xs1[a_idx]
                    b = xs1[b_idx]
                    for c_idx in range(len(xs2)):
                        for d_idx in range(c_idx+1,len(xs2)):
                            c = xs2[c_idx]
                            d = xs2[d_idx]
                            if a+b == c+d:
                                # found forbidden
                                return True, (y1,a,b,y2,c,d)
    return False, None

has_forbidden(rows)

[CODE] (turn 12):
def max_points_n(n):
    N = n*n
    # variable index mapping (x,y) -> idx
    idx = {}
    var = 0
    for y in range(1,n+1):
        for x in range(1,n+1):
            idx[(x,y)] = var
            var+=1
    c = -np.ones(N)  # maximize sum => minimize negative
    # constraints list
    A = []
    b = []
    # generate constraints
    for y1 in range(1,n+1):
        for y2 in range(y1+1,n+1):
            # list of columns
            cols = list(range(1,n+1))
            # precompute sums for pairs in each row
            pairs_y1 = [(a,b) for a in cols for b in cols if a<b]
            pairs_y2 = [(c,d) for c in cols for d in cols if c<d]
            # map sum to pairs for row2 maybe
            sum_to_pairs_y2 = {}
            for c,d in pairs_y2:
                s = c+d
                sum_to_pairs_y2.setdefault(s, []).append((c,d))
            for a,b in pairs_y1:
                s = a+b
                if s in sum_to_pairs_y2:
                    for c,d in sum_to_pairs_y2[s]:
                        # constraint: x_{a,y1}+x_{b,y1}+x_{c,y2}+x_{d,y2} <= 3
                        row = np.zeros(N, dtype=int)
                        row[idx[(a,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(b,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(c,y2)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(d,y2)]] = 1
                        A.append(row)
                        b.append(3)
    A = np.array(A)
    b = np.array(b)
    # bounds 0-1 integer
    bounds = Bounds(0, 1)
    # integrality vector
    integrality = np.ones(N, dtype=int)
    # Solve MILP
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, b), bounds=bounds,
               integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True})
    return -res.fun, res.x

max_val, sol = max_points_n(5)
max_val

[CODE] (turn 12):
def max_points_n(n):
    N = n*n
    # variable index mapping (x,y) -> idx
    idx = {}
    var = 0
    for y in range(1,n+1):
        for x in range(1,n+1):
            idx[(x,y)] = var
            var+=1
    c = -np.ones(N)  # maximize sum => minimize negative
    # constraints list
    A = []
    b = []
    # generate constraints
    for y1 in range(1,n+1):
        for y2 in range(y1+1,n+1):
            # list of columns
            cols = list(range(1,n+1))
            # precompute sums for pairs in each row
            pairs_y1 = [(a,b) for a in cols for b in cols if a<b]
            pairs_y2 = [(c,d) for c in cols for d in cols if c<d]
            # map sum to pairs for row2 maybe
            sum_to_pairs_y2 = {}
            for c,d in pairs_y2:
                s = c+d
                sum_to_pairs_y2.setdefault(s, []).append((c,d))
            for a,b in pairs_y1:
                s = a+b
                if s in sum_to_pairs_y2:
                    for c,d in sum_to_pairs_y2[s]:
                        # constraint: x_{a,y1}+x_{b,y1}+x_{c,y2}+x_{d,y2} <= 3
                        row = np.zeros(N, dtype=int)
                        row[idx[(a,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(b,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(c,y2)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(d,y2)]] = 1
                        A.append(row)
                        b.append(3)
    A = np.array(A)
    b = np.array(b)
    # bounds 0-1 integer
    bounds = Bounds(0, 1)
    # integrality vector
    integrality = np.ones(N, dtype=int)
    # Solve MILP
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, b), bounds=bounds,
               integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True})
    return -res.fun, res.x

max_val, sol = max_points_n(5)

def max_points_n(n):
    N = n*n
    idx = {}
    var = 0
    for y in range(1,n+1):
        for x in range(1,n+1):
            idx[(x,y)] = var
            var+=1
    c = -np.ones(N)  # maximize sum => minimize negative
    A = []
    b = []
    for y1 in range(1,n+1):
        for y2 in range(y1+1,n+1):
            cols = list(range(1,n+1))
            pairs_y1 = [(a,b) for a in cols for b in cols if a<b]
            pairs_y2 = [(c,d) for c in cols for d in cols if c<d]
            sum_to_pairs_y2 = {}
            for c,d in pairs_y2:
                s = c+d
                sum_to_pairs_y2.setdefault(s, []).append((c,d))
            for a,b in pairs_y1:
                s = a+b
                if s in sum_to_pairs_y2:
                    for c,d in sum_to_pairs_y2[s]:
                        row = np.zeros(N, dtype=int)
                        row[idx[(a,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(b,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(c,y2)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(d,y2)]] = 1
                        A.append(row)
                        b.append(3)
    A = np.array(A)
    b = np.array(b)
    bounds = Bounds(0, 1)
    integrality = np.ones(N, dtype=int)
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, b), bounds=bounds,
               integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True})
    return -res.fun, res.x

def max_points_n(n):

            pairs_y2 = [(c,d) for c in cols for d in cols if c<d]

import itertools, math

def is_valid_subset(subset, n):
    # subset is set of (x,y)
    # create dict rows->list of x
    rows = {}
    for (x,y) in subset:
        rows.setdefault(y, []).append(x)
    for y1, xs1 in rows.items():
        if len(xs1) < 2:
            continue
        xs1 = sorted(xs1)
        for y2, xs2 in rows.items():
            if y2 <= y1 or len(xs2) < 2:
                continue
            xs2 = sorted(xs2)
            # compute sums
            sums1 = {}
            for i in range(len(xs1)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(xs1)):
                    s = xs1[i] + xs1[j]
                    sums1.setdefault(s, []).append((xs1[i], xs1[j]))
            sums2 = {}
            for i in range(len(xs2)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(xs2)):
                    s = xs2[i] + xs2[j]
                    sums2.setdefault(s, []).append((xs2[i], xs2[j]))
            # check intersection
            for s in sums1:
                if s in sums2:
                    return False
    return True

def max_size_bruteforce(n):
    points = [(x,y) for y in range(1,n+1) for x in range(1,n+1)]
    maxsize = 0
    best = None
    for size in range(n*n, -1, -1):
        # iterate subsets of this size; break when found valid
        for subset in itertools.combinations(points, size):
            if is_valid_subset(subset, n):
                return size, subset
    return 0, None

maxsize, subset = max_size_bruteforce(5)
maxsize

import itertools, math

def is_valid_subset(subset, n):
    # subset is set of (x,y)
    # create dict rows->list of x
    rows = {}
    for (x,y) in subset:
        rows.setdefault(y, []).append(x)
    for y1, xs1 in rows.items():

            continue

            # compute sums

                    sums1.setdefault(s, []).append((xs1[i], xs1[j]))
            sums2 = {}

                for j in range(i+1, len(xs2)):

                    sums2.setdefault(s, []).append((xs2[i], xs2[j]))
            # check intersection
            for s in sums1:
                if s in sums2:
                    return False
    return True

def max_size_bruteforce(n):
    points = [(x,y) for y in range(1,n+1) for x in range(1,n+1)]
    maxsize = 0
    best = None
    for size in range(n*n, -1, -1):
        # iterate subsets of this size; break when found valid
        for subset in itertools.combinations(points, size):
            if is_valid_subset(subset, n):
                return size, subset
    return 0, None

[OUTPUT] (turn 17):
Mixed-integer linear programming

    Solves problems of the following form:

    .. math::

        \min_x \ & c^T x \\
        \mbox{such that} \ & b_l \leq A x \leq b_u,\\
        & l \leq x \leq u, \\
        & x_i \in \mathbb{Z}, i \in X_i

    where :math:`x` is a vector of decision variables;
    :math:`c`, :math:`b_l`, :math:`b_u`, :math:`l`, and :math:`u` are vectors;
    :math:`A` is a matrix, and :math:`X_i` is the set of indices of
    decision variables that must be integral. (

[CODE] (turn 18):

[OUTPUT] (turn 18):
In this context, a
    variable that can assume only integer values is said to be "integral";
    it has an "integrality" constraint.)

    Alternatively, that's:

    minimize::

        c @ x

    such that::

        b_l <= A @ x <= b_u
        l <= x <= u
        Specified elements of x must be integers

    By default, ``l = 0`` and ``u = np.inf`` unless specified with
    ``bounds``.

    Parameters
    ----------
    c : 1D dense array_like
        The coefficients of the linear objective

function to be minimized.
        `c` is converted to a double precision array before the problem is

        Indicates the type of integrality constraint on each decision variable.

        ``0`` : Continuous variable; no integrality constraint.

        ``1`` : Integer variable; decision variable must be an integer
        within `bounds`.

        ``2`` : Semi-continuous variable; decision variable must be within

def max_points_n_milp(n):
    N = n*n
    idx = {}
    var = 0
    for y in range(1,n+1):
        for x in range(1,n+1):
            idx[(x,y)] = var
            var+=1
    c = -np.ones(N)  # maximize sum
    A = []
    rhs = []
    for y1 in range(1,n+1):
        for y2 in range(y1+1,n+1):
            cols = list(range(1,n+1))
            # precompute pairs for each row
            pairs_y1 = [(a,b) for a in cols for b in cols if a<b]
            pairs_y2 = [(c,d) for c in cols for d in cols if c<d]
            sum_to_pairs_y2 = {}
            for c,d in pairs_y2:
                s = c+d
                sum_to_pairs_y2.setdefault(s, []).append((c,d))
            for a,b in pairs_y1:
                s = a+b
                if s in sum_to_pairs_y2:
                    for c,d in sum_to_pairs_y2[s]:
                        row = np.zeros(N, dtype=int)
                        row[idx[(a,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(b,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(c,y2)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(d,y2)]] = 1
                        A.append(row)
                        rhs.append(3)
    A = np.array(A, dtype=int)
    rhs = np.array(rhs, dtype=int)
    bounds = Bounds(0, 1)
    integrality = np.ones(N, dtype=int)
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, rhs), bounds=bounds,
               integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True})
    return -res.fun, res.x

max_val, sol = max_points_n_milp(5)

def max_points_n_milp(n):
    N = n*n
    idx = {}
    var = 0
    for y in range(1,n+1):
        for x in range(1,n+1):
            idx[(x,y)] = var
            var+=1
    c = -np.ones(N)  # maximize sum
    A = []
    rhs = []
    for y1 in range(1,n+1):
        for y2 in range(y1+1,n+1):
            cols = list(range(1,n+1))
            # precompute pairs for each row
            pairs_y1 = [(a,b) for a in cols for b in cols if a<b]
            pairs_y2 = [(c,d) for c in cols for d in cols if c<d]
            sum_to_pairs_y2 = {}
            for c,d in pairs_y2:
                s = c+d
                sum_to_pairs_y2.setdefault(s, []).append((c,d))
            for a,b in pairs_y1:
                s = a+b
                if s in sum_to_pairs_y2:
                    for c,d in sum_to_pairs_y2[s]:
                        row = np.zeros(N, dtype=int)
                        row[idx[(a,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(b,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(c,y2)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(d,y2)]] = 1
                        A.append(row)
                        rhs.append(3)
    A = np.array(A, dtype=int)
    rhs = np.array(rhs, dtype=int)
    bounds = Bounds(0, 1)
    integrality = np.ones(N, dtype=int)
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=LinearConstraint(A, -np.inf, rhs), bounds=bounds,
               integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True})
    return -res.fun, res.x

    idx = {}

        for x in range(1,n+1):

    ub = []

def build_A(n):
    N = n*n
    idx = {}
    var = 0
    for y in range(1,n+1):
        for x in range(1,n+1):
            idx[(x,y)] = var
            var+=1
    rows = []
    for y1 in range(1,n+1):
        for y2 in range(y1+1,n+1):
            cols = list(range(1,n+1))
            pairs_y1 = [(a,b) for a in cols for b in cols if a<b]
            pairs_y2 = [(c,d) for c in cols for d in cols if c<d]
            sum_to_pairs_y2 = {}
            for c,d in pairs_y2:
                s = c+d
                sum_to_pairs_y2.setdefault(s, []).append((c,d))
            for a,b in pairs_y1:
                s = a+b
                if s in sum_to_pairs_y2:
                    for c,d in sum_to_pairs_y2[s]:
                        row = np.zeros(N, dtype=int)
                        row[idx[(a,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(b,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(c,y2)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(d,y2)]] = 1
                        rows.append(row)
    A = np.array(rows, dtype=int)
    return A

    idx = {}

[CODE] (turn 36):
def is_valid_subset_full(subset, n):
    # subset set of (x,y)
    rows = {}
    cols = {}
    for (x,y) in subset:
        rows.setdefault(y, []).append(x)
        cols.setdefault(x, []).append(y)
    # check rows (horizontal bases)
    for y1, xs1 in rows.items():
        if len(xs1) < 2:
            continue
        xs1 = sorted(xs1)
        for y2, xs2 in rows.items():
            if y2 <= y1 or len(xs2) < 2:
                continue
            xs2 = sorted(xs2)
            sums1 = set()
            for i in range(len(xs1)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(xs1)):
                    sums1.add(xs1[i]+xs1[j])
            sums2 = set()
            for i in range(len(xs2)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(xs2)):
                    sums2.add(xs2[i]+xs2[j])
            if sums1 & sums2:
                return False
    # check columns (vertical bases)
    for x1, ys1 in cols.items():
        if len(ys1) < 2:
            continue
        ys1 = sorted(ys1)
        for x2, ys2 in cols.items():
            if x2 <= x1 or len(ys2) < 2:
                continue
            ys2 = sorted(ys2)
            sums1 = set()
            for i in range(len(ys1)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(ys1)):
                    sums1.add(ys1[i]+ys1[j])
            sums2 = set()
            for i in range(len(ys2)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(ys2)):
                    sums2.add(ys2[i]+ys2[j])
            if sums1 & sums2:
                return False
    return True

    rows, total = construct(n)
    # rows dict y->list of x
    subset = set()
    for y, xs in rows.items():
        for x in xs:
            subset.add((x,y))
    return subset

def is_valid_subset_full(subset, n):
    # subset set of (x,y)
    rows = {}
    cols = {}
    for (x,y) in subset:
        rows.setdefault(y, []).append(x)
        cols.setdefault(x, []).append(y)
    # check rows (horizontal bases)
    for y1, xs1 in rows.items():
        if len(xs1) < 2:
            continue
        xs1 = sorted(xs1)
        for y2, xs2 in rows.items():
            if y2 <= y1 or len(xs2) < 2:
                continue
            xs2 = sorted(xs2)
            sums1 = set()
            for i in range(len(xs1)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(xs1)):
                    sums1.add(xs1[i]+xs1[j])
            sums2 = set()
            for i in range(len(xs2)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(xs2)):
                    sums2.add(xs2[i]+xs2[j])
            if sums1 & sums2:
                return False
    # check columns (vertical bases)
    for x1, ys1 in cols.items():
        if len(ys1) < 2:
            continue
        ys1 = sorted(ys1)
        for x2, ys2 in cols.items():
            if x2 <= x1 or len(ys2) < 2:
                continue
            ys2 = sorted(ys2)
            sums1 = set()
            for i in range(len(ys1)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(ys1)):
                    sums1.add(ys1[i]+ys1[j])
            sums2 = set()
            for i in range(len(ys2)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(ys2)):
                    sums2.add(ys2[i]+ys2[j])
            if sums1 & sums2:
                return False
    return True

[CODE] (turn 37):
def construct(n):
    rows_k3 = (n-3)//2
    rows_k2 = n - rows_k3
    group_starts = list(range(3, 2*n, 4))[:rows_k3]
    used_sums = set()
    rows = {}
    row_idx = 1
    for s in group_starts:
        a = (s-1)//2
        rows[row_idx] = [a, a+1, a+2]
        used_sums.update([s, s+1, s+2])
        row_idx += 1
    remaining_sums = [x for x in range(3, 2*n) if x not in used_sums]
    for r in remaining_sums:
        x = max(1, r - n)
        y = r - x
        if x == y:
            # adjust (should not happen)
            x += 1
            y -= 1
        rows[row_idx] = [x, y]
        row_idx += 1
    total = sum(len(cols) for cols in rows.values()
    return rows, total

def construct_set(n):
    rows, total = construct(n)
    subset = set()
    for y, xs in rows.items():
        for x in xs:
            subset.add((x,y))
    return subset

subset5 = construct_set(5)
print(len(subset5))
print(is_valid_subset_full(subset5,5))

def construct(n):
    rows_k3 = (n-3)//2
    rows_k2 = n - rows_k3
    group_starts = list(range(3, 2*n, 4))[:rows_k3]
    used_sums = set()
    rows = {}
    row_idx = 1
    for s in group_starts:
        a = (s-1)//2
        rows[row_idx] = [a, a+1, a+2]
        used_sums.update([s, s+1, s+2])
        row_idx += 1
    remaining_sums = [x for x in range(3, 2*n) if x not in used_sums]
    for r in remaining_sums:
        x = max(1, r - n)
        y = r - x
        if x == y:
            x += 1
            y -= 1
        rows[row_idx] = [x, y]
        row_idx += 1
    total = sum(len(cols) for cols in rows.values())
    return rows, total

def construct_set(n):
    rows, total = construct(n)
    subset = set()
    for y, xs in rows.items():
        for x in xs:
            subset.add((x,y))
    return subset

def is_valid_subset_full(subset, n):
    rows = {}
    cols = {}
    for (x,y) in subset:
        rows.setdefault(y, []).append(x)
        cols.setdefault(x, []).append(y)
    # check rows
    for y1, xs1 in rows.items():
        if len(xs1) < 2:
            continue
        xs1 = sorted(xs1)
        for y2, xs2 in rows.items():
            if y2 <= y1 or len(xs2) < 2:
                continue
            xs2 = sorted(xs2)
            sums1 = set(xs1[i]+xs1[j] for i in range(len(xs1)) for j in range(i+1, len(xs1)))
            sums2 = set(xs2[i]+xs2[j] for i in range(len(xs2)) for j in range(i+1, len(xs2)))
            if sums1 & sums2:
                return False
    # check columns
    for x1, ys1 in cols.items():
        if len(ys1) < 2:
            continue
        ys1 = sorted(ys1)
        for x2, ys2 in cols.items():
            if x2 <= x1 or len(ys2) < 2:
                continue
            ys2 = sorted(ys2)
            sums1 = set(ys1[i]+ys1[j] for i in range(len(ys1)) for j in range(i+1, len(ys1)))
            sums2 = set(ys2[i]+ys2[j] for i in range(len(ys2)) for j in range(i+1, len(ys2)))
            if sums1 & sums2:
                return False
    return True

def construct(n):
    rows_k3 = (n-3)//2
    rows_k2 = n - rows_k3
    group_starts = list(range(3, 2*n, 4))[:rows_k3]
    used_sums = set()
    rows = {}
    row_idx = 1
    for s in group_starts:
        a = (s-1)//2
        rows[row_idx] = [a, a+1, a+2]
        used_sums.update([s, s+1, s+2])
        row_idx += 1
    remaining_sums = [x for x in range(3, 2*n) if x not in used_sums]
    for r in remaining_sums:
        x = max(1, r - n)
        y = r - x
        if x == y:
            x += 1
            y -= 1
        rows[row_idx] = [x, y]
        row_idx += 1
    total = sum(len(cols) for cols in rows.values())
    return rows, total

def max_points_both_orientations(n):
    N = n*n
    # variable index mapping (x,y) -> idx
    idx = {}
    var = 0
    for y in range(1,n+1):
        for x in range(1,n+1):
            idx[(x,y)] = var
            var+=1
    c = -np.ones(N)  # maximize sum
    rows_constraints = []
    cols_constraints = []
    # Row constraints (horizontal)
    for y1 in range(1,n+1):
        for y2 in range(y1+1,n+1):
            # pairs of columns
            for a in range(1,n+1):
                for b in range(a+1,n+1):
                    s = a+b
                    # find pairs (c,d) in row y2 with same sum
                    for ccol in range(1,n+1):
                        d = s - ccol
                        if d <= ccol or d > n:
                            continue
                        # ensure distinct columns? they can be same as a,b? That's okay as long as points distinct.
                        # Build constraint
                        row = np.zeros(N, dtype=int)
                        row[idx[(a,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(b,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(ccol,y2)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(d,y2)]] = 1
                        rows_constraints.append(row)
    # Column constraints (vertical)
    for x1 in range(1,n+1):
        for x2 in range(x1+1,n+1):
            for a in range(1,n+1):
                for b in range(a+1,n+1):
                    s = a+b
                    for crow in range(1,n+1):
                        drow = s - crow
                        if drow <= crow or drow > n:
                            continue
                        row = np.zeros(N, dtype=int)
                        row[idx[(x1,a)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(x1,b)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(x2,crow)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(x2,drow)]] = 1
                        cols_constraints.append(row)
    # Combine constraints
    A = np.vstack(rows_constraints + cols_constraints) if rows_constraints else np.empty((0,N), dtype=int)
    ub = np.full(A.shape[0], 3, dtype=int)
    lb = np.full(A.shape[0], -np.inf, dtype=float)
    bounds = Bounds(0,1)
    integrality = np.ones(N, dtype=int)
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=LinearConstraint(A, lb, ub), bounds=bounds,
               integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True})
    return -res.fun, res.x

max_val5, sol5 = max_points_both_orientations(5)
max_val5

def max_points_both_orientations(n):
    N = n*n
    # variable index mapping (x,y) -> idx
    idx = {}
    var = 0
    for y in range(1,n+1):
        for x in range(1,n+1):
            idx[(x,y)] = var
            var+=1
    c = -np.ones(N)  # maximize sum
    rows_constraints = []
    cols_constraints = []
    # Row constraints (horizontal)
    for y1 in range(1,n+1):
        for y2 in range(y1+1,n+1):
            # pairs of columns
            for a in range(1,n+1):
                for b in range(a+1,n+1):
                    s = a+b
                    # find pairs (c,d) in row y2 with same sum
                    for ccol in range(1,n+1):
                        d = s - ccol
                        if d <= ccol or d > n:
                            continue

                        # Build constraint

                        row[idx[(b,y1)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(ccol,y2)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(d,y2)]] = 1
                        rows_constraints.append(row)
    # Column constraints (vertical)
    for x1 in range(1,n+1):
        for x2 in range(x1+1,n+1):

                    s = a+b

                        if drow <= crow or drow > n:
                            continue

                        row[idx[(x1,a)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(x1,b)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(x2,crow)]] = 1
                        row[idx[(x2,drow)]] = 1
                        cols_constraints.append(row)
    # Combine constraints
    A = np.vstack(rows_constraints + cols_constraints) if rows_constraints else np.empty((0,N), dtype=int)

    integrality = np.ones(N, dtype=int)
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=LinearConstraint(A, lb, ub), bounds=bounds,
               integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True})
    return -res.fun, res.x
